## Corpus Check
- 1614 files · ~2,039,492 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 29266 nodes · 55666 edges · 1335 communities (1195 shown, 140 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 88% EXTRACTED · 12% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6864 edges (avg confidence: 0.74)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `dc145ab1`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
